### Self-service tenant register + workspace â€” all 6 slices released (2026-09-26)

Coordinator consolidation. All six claims `in-progress` -> `released`; the six
branches were file-disjoint and merged with **zero** collisions (verified by
building a 6x6 overlap matrix before the first merge).

| slice | commits | outcome |
|---|---|---|
| S | 4 | client_ip SSOT, Turnstile fail-closed, TESTING argv fix, nginx realip |
| A | 9 | reset-confirm 100% broken, password policy bypass, 500-after-commit, atomic rate limits, enumeration |
| B | 6 | cross-tenant `X-Tenant-ID` primitive, 2 NameErrors incl. a dead 429 path, AuditLogListView fail-closed, 4 resolvers -> 1 |
| C | 8 | deprovision FieldError, quota never enforced, outbox double-consumer, unique (tenant,name) |
| D | 6 | Turnstile site key unresolvable in Vite, duplicate top-level i18n `auth:` killing 40 keys/locale |
| E | 6 | tenant switch never reached the API, logout never revoked the PAT |

Plus two coordinator commits (`b24eca1c`, `96753858`): the 11 cross-slice gaps every
agent correctly refused to touch alone (settings/beat schedule, the A10 email
uniqueness migration, the onboarding exemption, the login case-sensitivity
lockout, i18n keys, `.d.ts`), and the response to an **independent** audit which
found 1 P0 (the client-IP fix had become a platform-wide DoS), 1 refuted claim
(two tenant resolvers still disagreed), 2 zero-coverage claims, and 3 P1s.

Honest status at close: **3 items deliberately left open**, each with a reason â€”
quota compares a per-DAY counter against a per-MONTH budget (~30x too permissive,
needs a product decision on the unit); `AuditLogListView` now fails closed, which
narrows the SOC operator's cross-tenant view; and the owner-only
`CoreTenant.objects.get(owner_user=...)` pattern in `commercial/views.py` still
500s for a member. Pre-existing red on `dev` and unchanged by this work:
`data_ingest.test_c9_*` (2, `No module named 'vnbroker'`) and
`vninvest_core`'s ~50 URL-routing failures.

Regex **khong neo** nen **bat ky dau `$` nao theo bang chu cai** cung bi coi la placeholder. Django hash dung `$` lam dau phan cach (`pbkdf2_sha256$600000$<salt>$<hash>`) nen **bi nuot im lang**. Chung minh bang A/B: cung mot shape, chi them mot dau `$` o giua â€” co `$` = LOT, khong `$` = BAT.; **NGUYEN NHAN GOC 2 â€” heuristic bo qua do chay lap.** `hasRepeatedBlock` khop `0000` trong `600000`. **KHONG nhoi heuristic nay** (se lam fixture lot), themi rule rieng high-confidence cho dinh dang hash.; **BAI CU: regex cua toi sai 2 lan, ca 2 lan deu do test/input tai, khong phai do scanner.** (1) Lop ky tu cua salt **thieu `=`** â€” salt base64 co padding, `[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}` khop het moi thu nhung that te khong khop gi. (2) **Dung `\b` lam guard** â€” bcrypt/argon2 **bat dau bang `$`** (ky tu non-word) nen `\b` khong bao gio gio duoc, hash nam dau dong (dung truong hop hay gap nhat) lai **bi bo loi**. Ca 2 lan deu bi self-test bat, khong phai bi review cua toi bat.; **SUA:** (1) neo `$NAME` vao dau gia tri, giu `${...}` khong neo; (2) rule moi `Password hash (Django/bcrypt/argon2)` danh dung `pbkdf2_sha256` / bcrypt / argon2, **doi ca hash day du** chu khong chi tien to, va co co `verbatim` de bo qua heuristic placeholder; (3) guard doi tu `\b` sang negative lookbehind.; **LOI CUA CHINH TOI, rule moi bat ngay:** `docs/CLEANUP_DECISIONS_20260928.md:31` (PR #57 da merge) **danh nguyen hash that vao khoi `sql`** khi mo ta buoc 1. **R13 kiem chung xong.** Do tren `vninvest-sdk` (FastAPI + Pydantic, khac han
Django). Do bang grep tren 355 file `.py`: **0** `import django` Â· **0**
`sqlalchemy` Â· **0** `models.Model` Â· **0** thu muc `migrations/`.
`vninvest_sdk/models.py:12` la **Pydantic** (`from pydantic import BaseModel,

**3 cap path:line truy nguoc that (R13 doi 3 cap):**
1. `vninvest-sdk/vninvest_fastapi/main.py:135-171` - `lifespan` dung
   singleton vao `app.state`; `add_middleware` theo thu tu **nguoc** =>
   chay `Auth -> Quota -> Metering`.
2. `middleware/auth.py:50` - `dispatch` **bo qua** `/health`, `/healthz`,

   **quyen gan o decorator**, khong phai o middleware.
4. `vninvest_sdk/client.py:5-6` - **dual-mode**: Gateway (SaaS) hoac Direct
   (bypass qua `vnbroker`).

**Khac `vninvest` o 3 diem cau truc, khong chi khac ngon ngu:**
(1) phan ra **theo domain** (`quote/company/finance/news/stream/admin`) x
cross-cutting (`auth/quota/metering`), khong phai theo tang MVC;
(2) phan quyen **hai noi** - middleware xac thuc, `Depends()` kiem scope;
(3) **dual-mode client** - cung mot route chay duoc ca SaaS va tu-host.

`_require_tenant_permission(request, 'billing:read')` **nam ngoai** `try`
nen 403 phat ra truoc. Gate cua ta la **lop thu hai** - giu duoc minh khi
wrapper doi. **Can claim rieng** de sua fail-open nay.

**Chua chay:** CI, `run_tests.py` toan bo, OpenAPI constitution/checklist.
